</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock" id="c556473798693271" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Income Taxes</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company accounts for income taxes under ASC Topic 740, &#8220;Income Taxes,&#8221; which requires an asset and liability approach to financial accounting and reporting for income taxes. Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are computed for differences between the consolidated financial statement and tax bases of assets and liabilities that will result in future taxable or deductible amounts, based on enacted tax laws and rates applicable to the periods in which the differences are expected to affect taxable income. Valuation allowances are established, when necessary, to reduce deferred tax assets to the amount expected to be realized.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">ASC Topic 740 prescribes a recognition threshold and a measurement attribute for the financial statement recognition and measurement of tax positions taken or expected to be taken in a tax return. For those benefits to be recognized, a tax position must be more likely than not to be sustained upon examination by taxing authorities. The Company&#8217;s management determined that the Cayman Islands is the Company&#8217;s major tax jurisdiction. The Company recognizes accrued interest and penalties related to unrecognized tax benefits as income tax expense. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, there were no unrecognized tax benefits and no amounts accrued for interest and penalties. The Company is currently not aware of any issues under review that could result in significant payments, accruals or material deviation from its position.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company is considered to be an exempted Cayman Islands company with no connection to any other taxable jurisdiction and is presently not subject to income taxes or income tax filing requirements in the Cayman Islands or the United States. As such, the Company&#8217;s tax provision was zero for the periods presented.</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock" id="c1049148074246927" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Net Income (Loss) per Ordinary Share</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company complies with the accounting and disclosure requirements of FASB ASC Topic 260, &#8220;Earnings Per Share&#8221;. Net income (loss) per ordinary share is computed by dividing net income (loss) by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding for the period. Accretion associated with the redeemable ordinary shares is excluded from earnings per share as the redemption value approximates fair value.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The calculation of diluted net income (loss) per share does not consider the effect of the warrants issued in connection with the (i) Initial Public Offering, and (ii) the private placement, since the exercise of the warrants are contingent upon the occurrence of future events. For the year ended December 31, 2025 and for the period from June 7, 2024 (inception) through December 31, 2024, the warrants are exercisable to purchase <ix:nonFraction id="c349" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11,920,000</ix:nonFraction> ordinary shares. The weighted average of these shares was excluded from the calculation of diluted net income (loss) per ordinary share since the inclusion of such warrants would be anti-dilutive. The warrants cannot be converted to ordinary shares prior to an initial Business Combination; therefore, they have been classified as anti-dilutive.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy" id="c248109352453520" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Fair Value of Financial Instruments</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under ASC&#160;820, &#8220;Fair&#160;Value Measurement,&#8221; approximates the carrying amounts represented in the consolidated balance sheets, primarily due to their short-term nature.</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock" id="c1423084375904791" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Fair Value Measurement</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s financial assets and liabilities reflects management&#8217;s estimate of amounts that the Company would have received in connection with the sale of the assets or paid in connection with the transfer of the liabilities in an orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement date. In connection with measuring the fair value of its assets and liabilities, the Company seeks to maximize the use of observable inputs (market data obtained from independent sources) and to minimize the use of unobservable inputs (internal assumptions about how market participants would price assets and liabilities). The following fair value hierarchy is used to classify assets and liabilities based on the observable inputs and unobservable inputs used in order to value the assets and liabilities:</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="width: 100%; text-indent: 0px;">

		<tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top;">
			<td style="width: 18pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: 36pt;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Level 1:</p>
			</td>
			<td style="width: auto;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Quoted prices in active markets for identical assets or liabilities. An active market for an asset or liability is a market in which transactions for the asset or liability occur with sufficient frequency and volume to provide pricing information on an ongoing basis.</p>
			</td>
		</tr>
		<tr style="vertical-align: top;">
			<td style="width: 18pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: 36pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: auto;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt;">&#160;</p>
			</td>
		</tr>
		<tr style="vertical-align: top;">
			<td style="width: 18pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: 36pt;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Level 2:</p>
			</td>
			<td style="width: auto;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Observable inputs other than Level 1 inputs. Examples of Level 2 inputs include quoted prices in active markets for similar assets or liabilities and quoted prices for identical assets or liabilities in markets that are not active.</p>
			</td>
		</tr>
		<tr style="vertical-align: top;">
			<td style="width: 18pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: 36pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: auto;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt;">&#160;</p>
			</td>
		</tr>
		<tr style="vertical-align: top;">
			<td style="width: 18pt;">&#160;</td>
			<td style="width: 36pt;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Level 3:</p>
			</td>
			<td style="width: auto;">
			<p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;font-size: 10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Unobservable inputs based on assessment of the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability.</p>
			</td>
		</tr>

</tbody></table>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>
<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityPolicyTextBlock" id="c400519398403491" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Warrant Instruments</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company accounted for the Public and Private Placement Warrants issued in connection with the Initial Public Offering and the private placement in accordance with the guidance contained in FASB ASC Topic&#160;815, &#8220;Derivatives and Hedging&#8221;, whereby under that provision, the warrants that do not meet the criteria for equity treatment must be recorded as liability. Accordingly, the Company evaluated and classified the warrant instruments under equity treatment at their assigned values.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<ix:exclude><div class="PGBK" style="width: 100%; margin-left: 0pt; margin-right: 0pt">

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="atiiu:TemporaryEquityPolicyTextBlock" id="c708883895856466" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Ordinary Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The public shares contain a redemption feature which allows for the redemption of such public shares in connection with the Company&#8217;s liquidation, or if there is a shareholder vote or tender offer in connection with the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination. In accordance with ASC 480-10-S99, the Company classifies public shares subject to possible redemption outside of permanent equity as the redemption provisions are not solely within the control of the Company. The Company recognizes changes in redemption value immediately as they occur and will adjust the carrying value of redeemable shares to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. Immediately upon the closing of the Initial Public Offering, the Company recognized the accretion from initial book value to redemption amount value. The change in the carrying value of redeemable shares will result in charges against additional paid-in capital (to the extent available) and accumulated deficit. Accordingly, as of December 31, 2025, the ordinary shares subject to possible redemption are presented at redemption value as temporary equity, outside of the shareholders&#8217; deficit section of the Company&#8217;s consolidated balance sheets. As of December 31, 2025, the ordinary shares subject to possible redemption reflected in the consolidated balance sheets are reconciled in the following table:</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 3</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>INITIAL PUBLIC OFFERING</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Pursuant to the Initial Public Offering, the Company sold <ix:nonFraction id="c457" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodShares" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">23,000,000</ix:nonFraction>&#160;Units, which included a full exercise by the underwriters of their over-allotment option of <ix:nonFraction id="c458" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-OverAllotmentOptionMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodShares" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction> Units, at a price of $<ix:nonFraction id="c459" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:UnitsIssuedPricePerUnit" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Unit. Each Unit consists of <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c460">one</span> ordinary share and <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c461">one</span>-half Public Warrant. Each whole Public Warrant entitles the holder to purchase <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c462">one</span> ordinary share at a price of $<ix:nonFraction id="c463" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11.50</ix:nonFraction> per share, subject to adjustment (Note&#160;7).</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<div>&#160;</div>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="atiiu:PrivatePlacementTextBlock" id="c464" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 4</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>PRIVATE PLACEMENT</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Simultaneously with the closing of the Initial Public Offering, the Company consummated the sale of an aggregate of <ix:nonFraction id="c467" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-PrivatePlacementMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodShares" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">840,000</ix:nonFraction> Private Placement Units at a price of $<ix:nonFraction id="c468" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-PrivatePlacementMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:UnitsIssuedPricePerUnit" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Private Placement Unit in a private placement to the Sponsor and BTIG, generating gross proceeds of $<ix:nonFraction id="c469" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-PrivatePlacementMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOrSaleOfEquity" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">8,400,000</ix:nonFraction>. Each Private Placement Unit consists of <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c470">one</span> ordinary share and <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c471">one</span>-half of one redeemable warrant (the &#8220;Private Placement Warrants&#8221;), with each whole Private Placement Warrant entitling the holder to purchase <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c472">one</span> ordinary share at a price of $<ix:nonFraction id="c473" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PrivatePlacementWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11.50</ix:nonFraction> per share, subject to adjustment (Note 7). The proceeds from the sale of the Private Placement Units are added to the net proceeds from the Initial Public Offering held in the Trust Account. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Completion Window, the proceeds from the sale of the Private Placement Units held in the Trust Account will be used to fund the redemption of the Public Shares (subject to the requirements of applicable law).</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 5</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>RELATED PARTIES</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Founder Shares</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">On June 7, 2024, the Sponsor made a capital contribution of $<ix:nonFraction id="c477" contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromContributedCapital" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">25,000</ix:nonFraction> to cover certain of the Company&#8217;s expenses, for which the Company issued <ix:nonFraction id="c478" contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">5,750,000</ix:nonFraction> ordinary shares (the &#8220;Founder Shares&#8221;). The Founder Shares include an aggregate of up to <ix:nonFraction id="c479" contextRef="i_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:SharesSubjectToForfeiture" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">750,000</ix:nonFraction> shares subject to surrender and forfeiture to the extent that the underwriters&#8217; over-allotment is not exercised in full or in part, so that the number of Founder Shares will equal, on an as-converted basis, approximately <ix:nonFraction id="c480" contextRef="i_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="Pure" name="atiiu:FounderSharesPercentageOfOutstandingStockAfterIPO" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="1">20</ix:nonFraction>% of the Company&#8217;s issued and outstanding ordinary shares after the Initial Public Offering. As a result of the Initial Public Offering and the full exercise by the underwriters of their over-allotment option, <ix:nonFraction id="c481" contextRef="i_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:SharesSubjectToForfeiture" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">750,000</ix:nonFraction> shares are no longer subject to surrender and forfeiture.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Pursuant to a letter agreement that the initial shareholders, directors and officers have entered into with the Company, with certain limited exceptions, the Founder Shares are not transferable, assignable or salable (except to directors and officers and other persons or entities affiliated with the initial shareholders, each of whom will be subject to the same transfer restrictions) until the earlier of: (i)&#160;<span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c482">six</span> months after the completion of the initial Business Combination; and (ii)&#160;subsequent to the initial Business Combination, the date on which the Company completes a liquidation, merger, share exchange, reorganization or other similar transaction that results in all of the public shareholders having the right to exchange their ordinary shares for cash, securities or other property. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the Founder Shares will be released from the lock-up if (1) the last reported sale price of the Company&#8217;s ordinary shares equals or exceeds $<ix:nonFraction id="c483" contextRef="i_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:FounderSharesLockupThresholdSharePrice" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">12.00</ix:nonFraction> per share (as adjusted for share splits, share capitalizations, reorganizations, recapitalizations and other similar transactions) for any <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" name="atiiu:FounderSharesLockupThresholdConsecutiveTradingDays" id="c484" format="ixt-sec:durday">20</ix:nonNumeric> trading days within any <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2024-06-07_2024-06-07_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" name="atiiu:FounderSharesLockupThresholdTradingDays" id="c485" format="ixt-sec:durday">30</ix:nonNumeric>-trading day period after the initial Business Combination or (2) if the Company completes a transaction after the initial Business Combination which results in all of the Company&#8217;s shareholders having the right to exchange their shares for cash, securities or other property.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Promissory Note</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>Related Party</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">On June 7, 2024, the Sponsor issued an unsecured promissory note to the Company (as amended, the &#8220;Promissory Note&#8221;), pursuant to which the Company may borrow up to an aggregate principal amount of $<ix:nonFraction id="c486" contextRef="i_2024-06-07_DebtInstrumentAxis-PromissoryNoteMember_LineOfCreditFacilityAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="atiiu:DebtInstrumentMaximumAmount" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">290,000</ix:nonFraction>. The Promissory Note was non-interest bearing and payable on the earlier of (i) March&#160;31, 2025 or (ii)&#160;the consummation of the Initial Public Offering or (iii) the date on which the Company determines to not proceed with the Initial Public Offering. Simultaneously with the closing of the Initial Public Offering on February 12, 2025, the Company repaid the outstanding borrowings under the Promissory Note amounting to $<ix:nonFraction id="c487" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_DebtInstrumentAxis-PromissoryNoteMember_LineOfCreditFacilityAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RepaymentsOfRelatedPartyDebt" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">290,000</ix:nonFraction>. Borrowings under the note are no longer available. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, the Company had $<ix:nonFraction id="c488" contextRef="i_2025-12-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-PromissoryNoteMember_LineOfCreditFacilityAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">0</ix:nonFraction> and $<ix:nonFraction id="c489" contextRef="i_2024-12-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-PromissoryNoteMember_LineOfCreditFacilityAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">192,033</ix:nonFraction> outstanding under the Promissory Note, respectively.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt;"><b>Due to Related Party</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family: Times New Roman; font-size: 10pt; font-variant: normal; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt;">Due to related party represents recurring Company expenses advanced by officers on behalf of the Company. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, the Company owed $1,239 and $0 to officers, respectively.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Administrative Support Agreement</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Commencing on the effective date, February 10, 2025, the Company agreed to reimburse the Sponsor in an amount equal to $<ix:nonFraction id="c490" contextRef="i_2025-02-10_RelatedPartyTransactionAxis-OfficeSpaceAdministrativeAndSupportServicesMember_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="atiiu:MaximumMonthlyReimbursement" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">10,000</ix:nonFraction> per month for office space, administrative and support services. Upon completion of the initial Business Combination or the Company&#8217;s liquidation, the Company will cease paying these monthly fees. For the year ended December 31, 2025, the Company incurred and paid $<ix:nonFraction id="c491" contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31_RelatedPartyTransactionAxis-OfficeSpaceAdministrativeAndSupportServicesMember_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-ArchimedesTechSpacSponsorsIiLlcTheSponsorMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:RelatedPartyTransactionAmountsOfTransaction" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">106,786</ix:nonFraction> in fees for these services. For the period from June 7, 2024 (inception) through December 31, 2024, the Company did <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c492">not</span> incur any fees for these services.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Working Capital Loans</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">In order to finance transaction costs in connection with a Business Combination, the Sponsor or an affiliate of the Sponsor, or certain of the Company&#8217;s officers and directors may, but are not obligated to, loan the Company funds as may be required (the &#8220;Working Capital Loans&#8221;). If the Company completes a Business Combination, the Company would repay the Working Capital Loans out of the proceeds of the Trust Account released to the Company. Otherwise, the Working Capital Loans would be repaid only out of funds held outside the Trust Account. In the event that a Business Combination does not close, the Company may use a portion of proceeds held outside the Trust Account to repay the Working Capital Loans, but no proceeds held in the Trust Account would be used to repay the Working Capital Loans. Except for the foregoing, the terms of such Working Capital Loans, if any, have not been determined and no written agreements exist with respect to such loans. The Working Capital Loans would either be repaid upon consummation of a Business Combination, without interest, or, at the lender&#8217;s discretion, up to $<ix:nonFraction id="c493" contextRef="i_2025-12-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-WorkingCapitalLoansMember_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-RelatedPartyMember" unitRef="USD" name="atiiu:DebtInstrumentMaximumAmount" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction> of such Working Capital Loans may be convertible into units of the post-Business Combination entity at a price of $<ix:nonFraction id="c494" contextRef="i_2025-12-31_DebtInstrumentAxis-WorkingCapitalLoansMember_RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis-RelatedPartyMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per unit. The units would be identical to the Private Placement Units. As of December 31, 2025 and December 31, 2024, <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c495"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c496">no</span></span> Working Capital Loans were outstanding.</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 6</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCIES</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Risks and Uncertainties</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company&#8217;s ability to complete an initial Business Combination may be adversely affected by various factors, many of which are beyond the Company&#8217;s control. The Company&#8217;s ability to consummate an initial Business Combination could be impacted by, among other things, changes in laws or regulations, downturns in the financial markets or in economic conditions, inflation, fluctuations in interest rates, increases in tariffs, supply chain disruptions, declines in consumer confidence and spending, public health considerations, and geopolitical instability, such as the military conflicts in Ukraine and the Middle East. The Company cannot at this time predict the likelihood of one or more of the above events, their duration or magnitude or the extent to which they may negatively impact the Company&#8217;s ability to complete an initial Business Combination.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Registration Rights</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The holders of the Founder Shares, Private Placement Units (and underlying securities) and any units (and underlying securities) that may be issued on conversion of Working Capital Loans are entitled to registration rights pursuant to a registration rights agreement requiring the Company to register such securities for resale. The holders of these securities are entitled to make up to three demands, excluding short form registration demands, that the Company register such securities. In addition, the holders have certain piggyback registration rights with respect to registration statements filed subsequent to the completion of the initial Business Combination and rights to require the Company to register for resale such securities pursuant to Rule 415 under the Securities Act. However, the registration rights agreement provides that the Company will not be required to effect or permit any registration or cause any registration statement to become effective until termination of the applicable lock-up period. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection with the filing of any such registration statements.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Underwriting Agreement</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company granted the underwriters a <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-OverAllotmentOptionMember" name="atiiu:EquityOfferingOptionPeriod" id="c500" format="ixt-sec:durday">45</ix:nonNumeric>-day option from the date of Initial Public Offering to purchase up to <ix:nonFraction id="c501" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-OverAllotmentOptionMember" unitRef="Share" name="atiiu:EquityOfferingOptionMaximumSharesToBeIssued" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction> additional Units&#160;to cover over-allotments, if any, at the Initial Public Offering price less the underwriting discounts and commissions. On February 12, 2025, simultaneously with the Initial Public Offering, the underwriters exercised their over-allotment option in full, closing on the <ix:nonFraction id="c502" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-OverAllotmentOptionMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction> additional Units.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The underwriters were paid a cash underwriting discount of $<ix:nonFraction id="c503" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_CounterpartyNameAxis-UnderwritersMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:EquityOfferingCashUnderwritingFeePerUnit" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.20</ix:nonFraction> per Unit, or $<ix:nonFraction id="c504" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_CounterpartyNameAxis-UnderwritersMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:PaymentsOfStockIssuanceCosts" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">4,600,000</ix:nonFraction> in the aggregate upon the closing of the Initial Public Offering. In addition, the underwriters are entitled to a deferred fee of $<ix:nonFraction id="c505" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_CounterpartyNameAxis-UnderwritersMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:EquityOfferingDeferredUnderwritingFeePerUnit" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.35</ix:nonFraction>&#160;per Unit, or $<ix:nonFraction id="c506" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_CounterpartyNameAxis-UnderwritersMember_SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis-IPOMember" unitRef="USD" name="us-gaap:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInCapitalStockIssuedIssuanceCosts" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="0">8,050,000</ix:nonFraction> in the aggregate. The deferred fee will become payable to the underwriters from the amounts held in the Trust Account solely in the event that the Company completes a Business Combination, subject to the terms of the underwriting agreement.</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:StockholdersEquityNoteDisclosureTextBlock" id="c507" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 7</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;<b>SHAREHOLDERS</b>&#8217; <b>DEFICIT</b></p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Preference Shares</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;The Company is authorized to issue <ix:nonFraction id="c510" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:PreferredStockSharesAuthorized" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">1,000,000</ix:nonFraction> preference shares with a par value of $<ix:nonFraction id="c511" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:PreferredStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.0001</ix:nonFraction> per share with such designations, voting and other rights and preferences as may be determined from time to time by the Company&#8217;s board of directors. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, there were <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c512"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c513"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c514"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c515">no</span></span></span></span> preference shares issued or outstanding.</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Ordinary Shares</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;The Company is authorized to issue <ix:nonFraction id="c516" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesAuthorized" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">400,000,000</ix:nonFraction> ordinary shares with a par value of $<ix:nonFraction id="c517" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">0.0001</ix:nonFraction> per share. Holders of ordinary shares are entitled to one vote for each share. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, there were <ix:nonFraction id="c518" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesIssued" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF"><ix:nonFraction id="c519" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">6,590,000</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction> and <ix:nonFraction id="c520" contextRef="i_2024-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesIssued" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF"><ix:nonFraction id="c521" contextRef="i_2024-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">5,750,000</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction> ordinary shares issued and outstanding, excluding the <ix:nonFraction id="c522" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">23,000,000</ix:nonFraction> and <ix:nonFraction id="c523" contextRef="i_2024-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="-3">0</ix:nonFraction> ordinary shares subject to possible redemption, respectively.</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><b>Warrants</b>&#160;&#8212;&#160;As of December 31, 2025, there were <ix:nonFraction id="c524" contextRef="i_2025-12-31" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11,920,000</ix:nonFraction> warrants outstanding, including <ix:nonFraction id="c525" contextRef="i_2025-12-31_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">11,500,000</ix:nonFraction> Public Warrants and <ix:nonFraction id="c526" contextRef="i_2025-12-31_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PrivatePlacementWarrantsMember" unitRef="Share" name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightOutstanding" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">420,000</ix:nonFraction> Private Placement Warrants. There are no warrants issued or outstanding as of December 31, 2024. Public Warrants may only be exercised for a whole number of shares. No fractional warrants will be issued upon separation of the Units&#160;and only whole warrants will trade. The Public Warrants will become exercisable on the later of (a)&#160;<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsExercisablePeriodAfterBusinessCombination" id="c527" format="ixt-sec:durday">30</ix:nonNumeric>&#160;days after the completion of a Business Combination and (b)&#160;<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsExercisablePeriodAfterIPO" id="c528" format="ixt-sec:durmonth">12</ix:nonNumeric>&#160;months from the closing of the Initial Public Offering. The Public Warrants will expire <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c529">five</span>&#160;years after the completion of a Business Combination or earlier upon redemption or liquidation.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company will not be obligated to deliver any ordinary shares pursuant to the exercise of a warrant and will have no obligation to settle such warrant exercise unless a registration statement under the Securities Act covering the issuance of the ordinary shares underlying the warrants is then effective and a prospectus relating thereto is current, subject to the Company satisfying its obligations with respect to registration. No warrant will be exercisable and the Company will not be obligated to issue ordinary shares upon exercise of a warrant unless ordinary shares issuable upon such warrant exercise has been registered, qualified or deemed to be exempt under the securities laws of the state of residence of the registered holder of the warrants.</p>

<ix:exclude><p style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; margin: 0pt; text-align: left">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company has agreed that as soon as practicable, but in no event later than twenty (<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsMaximumPeriodAfterBusinessAcquisitionProspectusIssued" id="c530" format="ixt-sec:durday">20</ix:nonNumeric>) business days after the closing of a Business Combination, it will use its best efforts to file with the SEC a registration statement covering the ordinary share issuable upon exercise of the warrants, to cause such registration statement to become effective and to maintain a current prospectus relating to those ordinary shares until the warrants expire or are redeemed, as specified in the warrant agreement. If a registration statement covering the ordinary shares issuable upon exercise of the warrants is not effective by the <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsMaximumPeriodAfterBusinessCombinationFailureToFileProspectusCashlessExercise" id="c531" format="ixt-sec:durday">60</ix:nonNumeric>th business&#160;day after the closing of a Business Combination, warrant holders may, until such time as there is an effective registration statement and during any period when the Company will have failed to maintain an effective registration statement, exercise warrants on a &#8220;cashless basis&#8221; in accordance with Section&#160;3(a)(9)&#160;of the Securities Act or another exemption. Notwithstanding the foregoing, if a registration statement covering the ordinary shares issuable upon exercise of the warrants is not effective within a specified period following the consummation of a Business Combination, warrant holders may, until such time as there is an effective registration statement and during any period when the Company shall have failed to maintain an effective registration statement, exercise warrants on a cashless basis pursuant to the exemption provided by Section&#160;3(a)(9)&#160;of the Securities Act, provided that such exemption is available. If that exemption, or another exemption, is not available, holders will not be able to exercise their warrants on a cashless basis.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Once the warrants become exercisable, the Company may redeem the outstanding Public Warrants:</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">If the Company calls the warrants for redemption, management will have the option to require all holders that wish to exercise warrants to do so on a &#8220;cashless basis.&#8221; In determining whether to require all holders to exercise their warrants on a &#8220;cashless basis,&#8221; management will consider, among other factors, the Company&#8217;s cash position, the number of warrants that are outstanding and the dilutive effect on the Company&#8217;s shareholders of issuing the maximum number of ordinary shares issuable upon the exercise of the Company&#8217;s warrants. In such event, each holder would pay the exercise price by surrendering the warrants for that number of ordinary shares equal to the quotient obtained by dividing (x) the product of the number of ordinary shares underlying the warrants, multiplied by the excess of the &#8220;fair market value&#8221; over the exercise price of the warrants by (y) the fair market value. The &#8220;fair market value&#8221; shall mean the highest closing sale price of the ordinary share for the <ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" name="atiiu:ClassOfWarrantOrRightCashlessRedemptionThresholdTradingDays" id="c538" format="ixt-sec:durday">10</ix:nonNumeric> trading days ending on the third trading day prior to the date on which the notice of redemption is sent to the holders of warrants.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">In addition, if (x) the Company issues additional ordinary shares or equity-linked securities for capital raising purposes in connection with the closing of the initial Business Combination at a Newly Issued Price of less than $<ix:nonFraction id="c539" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsAdjustmentOfExercisePriceMinimumPriceOfIssuedSharesInBusinessAcquisition" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">9.20</ix:nonFraction> per ordinary shares (with such issue price or effective issue price to be determined in good faith by the board of directors and, in the case of any such issuance to the Sponsor or its affiliates, without taking into account any Founder Shares held by the Sponsor or such affiliates, as applicable, prior to such issuance), (y) the aggregate gross proceeds from such issuances represent more than <ix:nonFraction id="c540" contextRef="d_2025-02-12_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="Pure" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsAdjustmentOfExercisePriceMaximumPercentageOfEquityProceeds" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="1">60</ix:nonFraction>% of the total equity proceeds, and interest thereon, available for the funding of the initial Business Combination on the date of the consummation of the initial Business Combination (net of redemptions), and (z) the Market Value is below $<ix:nonFraction id="c541" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:WarrantsAndRightsAdjustmentOfExercisePriceMinimumMarketValue" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">9.20</ix:nonFraction> per share, then the exercise price of the warrants will be adjusted (to the nearest cent) to be equal to <ix:nonFraction id="c542" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="Pure" name="atiiu:ClassOfWarrantsOrRightsAdjustmentOfExercisePriceExercisePricePercentageOfMarketValueOrNewlyIssuedPrice" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="2">115</ix:nonFraction>% of the higher of the Market Value and the Newly Issued Price, and the $<ix:nonFraction id="c543" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="USDPerShare" name="atiiu:ClassOfWarrantOrRightRedemptionThresholdSharePrice" scale="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="INF">18.00</ix:nonFraction> per share redemption trigger price described above will be adjusted (to the nearest cent) to be equal to <ix:nonFraction id="c544" contextRef="i_2025-02-12_ClassOfWarrantOrRightAxis-PublicWarrantsMember" unitRef="Pure" name="atiiu:ClassOfWarrantsOrRightsAdjustmentOfExercisePriceRedemptionThresholdPercentageOfMarketValueOrNewlyIssuedPrice" scale="-2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" decimals="1">180</ix:nonFraction>% of the higher of the Market Value and the Newly Issued Price.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Private Placement Warrants will be identical to the Public Warrants underlying the Units&#160;being sold in the Initial Public Offering, subject to certain limited exceptions.&#160;</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<div>&#160;</div>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="d_2025-01-01_2025-12-31" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock" id="c545" escape="true">
<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;"><b>NOTE 8</b> &#8212; <b>SEGMENT INFORMATION</b>&#8239;&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">ASC Topic 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; establishes standards for companies to report in their financial statement information about operating segments, products, services, geographic areas, and major customers. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise that engage in business activities from which it may recognize revenues and incur expenses, and for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker (&#8220;CODM&#8221;), or group, in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company&#8217;s CODM has been identified as the Chief Executive Officer, who reviews the assets, operating results, and financial metrics for the Company as a whole to make decisions about allocating resources and assessing financial performance. Accordingly, management has determined that there is only <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:c548">one</span> reportable segment.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<ix:exclude><div class="PGBK" style="width: 100%; margin-left: 0pt; margin-right: 0pt">
<div class="PGFTR" style="text-align: center; width: 100%">
<div class="hf-row">
<div class="hf-cell PGNUM" style="text-align: center; font-size: 10pt; font-family: Times New Roman;">F-16</div>
</div>
</div>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;"><a id="endnotes" href="#"></a>The CODM assesses performance for the single segment and decides how to allocate resources based on net income or loss that also is reported on the consolidated statements of operations as net income or loss. The measure of segment assets is reported on the consolidated balance sheets as total assets. When evaluating the Company&#8217;s performance and making key decisions regarding resource allocation, the CODM reviews several key metrics included in net income or loss and total assets, which include the following:&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The CODM is provided with details of cash and liquid resources available with the Company. The CODM reviews the position of total assets available with the Company to assess if the Company has sufficient resources available to discharge its liabilities.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The CODM reviews interest earned on cash held in Trust Account to measure and monitor shareholder value and determine the most effective strategy of investment with the Trust Account funds while maintaining compliance with the Trust Agreement.</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">Interest earned on cash in bank account and general and administrative expenses are reviewed and monitored by the CODM to manage and forecast cash to ensure enough capital is available to complete a business combination or similar transaction within the business combination period. The CODM also reviews the interest earned on cash in bank account and general and administrative expenses to manage, maintain and enforce all contractual agreements to ensure costs are aligned with all agreements and budget. General and administrative expenses, as reported on the consolidated statements of operations, are the significant segment expenses provided to the CODM on a regular basis.&#160;&#160;</p>
</ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:'Times New Roman';font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;margin:0pt;">&#160;</p>

<p style="font-family:Times New Roman;font-size:10pt;font-variant:normal;text-align:justify;margin:0pt;">The Company evaluated subsequent events and transactions that occurred after the consolidated balance sheet date up to the date that the consolidated financial statements were issued. Based upon this review, the Company did not identify any subsequent events that would have required adjustment or disclosure in the consolidated financial statements.</p>
